# Skills · R · part 12

906 entries, rdk-ecosystem to rea. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **rdk-ecosystem**: RDK board selection — which board to buy (X3/X5/Ultra/S100/S100P/S600), whether YOLO/LLM/VLM will run, comparison with Jetson/Raspberry Pi/RK3588, LLM/VLM expectations, and official ecosystem entry points. - **rdk-embodied-lerobot**: Deploy trained LeRobot ACT or Pi0/openpi VLA policies on RDK S-series: export to ONNX, compile to BPU `.hbm`, and run the board control loop.
